def _build_token_timestamps(
    asr_result: ASRStreamResult,
    *,
    offset_seconds: float,
    max_end_seconds: float | None,
) -> list[TokenTimestamp]:
    """Map segment-relative ASR token end times to monotonic session-time timestamps."""
    if offset_seconds < 0:
        raise ValueError("offset_seconds must be non-negative")
    if max_end_seconds is not None and max_end_seconds < offset_seconds:
        raise ValueError("max_end_seconds must be >= offset_seconds")

    tokens: list[TokenTimestamp] = []
    end_times = asr_result.timestamps
    limit = min(len(asr_result.tokens), len(end_times))
    last_end = offset_seconds

    for index in range(limit):
        token = asr_result.tokens[index]

        segment_start = end_times[index - 1] if index > 0 else 0.0
        segment_end = end_times[index]
        session_start = max(0.0, segment_start) + offset_seconds
        session_end = max(segment_end, segment_start) + offset_seconds
        session_start = max(session_start, last_end)
        session_end = max(session_end, session_start)
        if max_end_seconds is not None:
            session_end = min(session_end, max_end_seconds)
            session_start = min(session_start, session_end)

        tokens.append(TokenTimestamp(text=token, start=session_start, end=session_end))
        last_end = session_end

    return tokens


def _to_sample_tokens(
    tokens: list[TokenTimestamp],
    *,
    sample_rate: int,
) -> list[SampleTokenTimestamp]:
    token_dicts = [{"text": t.text, "start": t.start, "end": t.end} for t in tokens]
    return tokens_seconds_to_samples(token_dicts, sample_rate=sample_rate)


class ASRModel(Protocol):
    def create_stream(self) -> ASRStream: ...


def _summarize_validation_error(exc: ValidationError, *, prefix: str) -> str:
    errors = exc.errors()
    if not errors:
        return prefix
    message = errors[0].get("msg") or prefix
    return f"{prefix}: {message}"


def _get_asr_model_for_language(
    language: str,
    *,
    model_manager: ModelManager | None,
    fallback_models: dict[str, ASRModel],
) -> ASRModel:
    if model_manager is not None:
        return model_manager.get(language)
    return fallback_models[language]


def _apply_language_switch(
    *,
    switch_event: LanguageSwitchDetectedEvent,
    session_state: SessionState,
    coordinator: Coordinator,
    rollback_engine: RollbackEngine,
    model_manager: ModelManager | None,
    fallback_models: dict[str, ASRModel],
    sample_rate: int,
    audio_end_sample: int,
    token_duration_tolerance: float,
) -> tuple[ASRModel, ASRStream, TranscriptionMessage | None]:
    session_state.switch_language(switch_event.to_language)
    asr_model = _get_asr_model_for_language(
        switch_event.to_language,
        model_manager=model_manager,
        fallback_models=fallback_models,
    )
    try:
        asr_stream = asr_model.create_stream()
    except Exception as exc:
        raise ComponentError("ASR", f"ASR stream initialization failed: {exc}") from exc
    coordinator.replace_asr_stream(asr_stream)

    rollback_engine.rollback(session_state, switch_event.boundary_sample)

    try:
        audio_bytes = session_state.audio_buffer.get_range(switch_event.boundary_sample, audio_end_sample)
    except ValueError as exc:
        logger.warning(
            "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=re_inference_skipped | "
            "reason=buffer_missing | start=%s | end=%s | error=%s",
            session_state.session_id,
            switch_event.boundary_sample,
            audio_end_sample,
            exc,
        )
        return asr_model, asr_stream, None

    if not audio_bytes:
        logger.warning(
            "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=re_inference_skipped | "
            "reason=empty_buffer | start=%s | end=%s | start_seconds=%s | end_seconds=%s",
            session_state.session_id,
            switch_event.boundary_sample,
            audio_end_sample,
            f"{(switch_event.boundary_sample / sample_rate):.3f}",
            f"{(audio_end_sample / sample_rate):.3f}",
        )
        return asr_model, asr_stream, None

    start_time = time.perf_counter()
    audio = np.frombuffer(audio_bytes, dtype=np.float32)
    asr_stream.accept_samples(audio)
    asr_stream.decode_available()
    asr_result = asr_stream.get_result()
    duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000)

    logger.info(
        "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=re_inference_complete | "
        "language=%s | sample_range=%s-%s | start_seconds=%s | end_seconds=%s | duration_ms=%s",
        session_state.session_id,
        switch_event.to_language,
        switch_event.boundary_sample,
        audio_end_sample,
        f"{(switch_event.boundary_sample / sample_rate):.3f}",
        f"{(audio_end_sample / sample_rate):.3f}",
        duration_ms,
    )

    if not (asr_result.text.strip() or asr_result.tokens):
        return asr_model, asr_stream, None

    segment_start_seconds = session_state.segment_start_sample / sample_rate
    end_seconds = audio_end_sample / sample_rate
    tokens = _build_token_timestamps(
        asr_result,
        offset_seconds=segment_start_seconds,
        max_end_seconds=end_seconds + token_duration_tolerance,
    )
    timestamp = tokens[0].start if tokens else segment_start_seconds
    message = TranscriptionMessage(
        type="partial",
        language=session_state.current_language,
        text=asr_result.text,
        timestamp=timestamp,
        tokens=tokens,
    )
    session_state.add_transcription(
        text=asr_result.text,
        type="partial",
        language=session_state.current_language,
        sample_start=session_state.segment_start_sample,
        tokens=_to_sample_tokens(tokens, sample_rate=sample_rate),
    )
    return asr_model, asr_stream, message


async def _send_error_and_close(
    websocket: WebSocket,
    *,
    code: str,
    message: str,
    session_id: str | None,
    unexpected: bool = False,
) -> None:
    logger.error(
        "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=error | code=%s | error=%s",
        session_id,
        code,
        message,
        exc_info=unexpected,
    )
    error = ErrorMessage(code=code, message=message)
    with suppress(Exception):
        await websocket.send_json(error.model_dump())
    with suppress(Exception):
        await websocket.close()


@router.websocket("/ws/transcribe")
async def transcribe_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
    """Real-time transcription session over WebSocket."""
    session_state: SessionState | None = None
    session_id: str | None = None
    coordinator: Coordinator | None = None
    lid_scheduler: ProgressiveLIDScheduler | None = None
    rollback_engine: RollbackEngine | None = None

    try:
        await websocket.accept()

        try:
            start_payload = await websocket.receive_json()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ConfigurationError("Invalid start message") from exc

        try:
            start = StartMessage.model_validate(start_payload)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            raise ConfigurationError(_summarize_validation_error(exc, prefix="Invalid start message")) from exc

        components = websocket.app.state.components
        model_manager = getattr(components, "asr_model_manager", None)
        supported_languages = (
            set(model_manager.languages()) if model_manager is not None else set(components.asr_models.keys())
        )
        requested_languages = set(start.languages)
        unsupported = sorted(requested_languages - supported_languages)
        if unsupported:
            if components.errors.get("asr"):
                raise ComponentError(
                    "ASR",
                    f"ASR model unavailable for: {', '.join(unsupported)}",
                )
            raise ConfigurationError(f"Unsupported languages: {', '.join(unsupported)}")

        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        logger.info(
            "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=session_started | languages=%s",
            session_id,
            start.languages,
        )

        sample_clock = SampleClock(sample_rate=16000)
        audio_buffer = AudioBuffer(session_id=session_id)
        settings = get_settings()
        language_switch_threshold = (
            start.language_switch_confidence_threshold
            if start.language_switch_confidence_threshold is not None
            else settings.language_switch_confidence_threshold
        )
        session_state = SessionState(
            session_id=session_id,
            languages=start.languages,
            vad_silence_duration=start.vad_silence_duration,
            language_switch_confidence_threshold=language_switch_threshold,
            sample_clock=sample_clock,
            audio_buffer=audio_buffer,
        )
        rollback_engine = RollbackEngine()
        if components.vad is None:
            raise ComponentError("VAD", "VAD component unavailable")
        vad_stream = components.vad.create_stream(vad_silence_duration=session_state.vad_silence_duration)
        if session_state.current_language not in supported_languages:
            raise ComponentError(
                "ASR",
                f"ASR model unavailable for: {session_state.current_language}",
            )
        asr_model = (
            model_manager.get(session_state.current_language)
            if model_manager is not None
            else components.asr_models[session_state.current_language]
        )
        try:
            asr_stream = asr_model.create_stream()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ComponentError("ASR", f"ASR stream initialization failed: {exc}") from exc
        coordinator = Coordinator(
            session_state,
            vad_stream=vad_stream,
            asr_stream=asr_stream,
            sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate,
        )
        language_switch_threshold = session_state.language_switch_confidence_threshold
        max_window_seconds = settings.progressive_windows[-1] if settings.progressive_windows else None

        def _handle_lid_result(event: LIDResultEvent) -> None:
            if session_state is None:
                return

            session_state.record_lid_result(event)

            switch_event = None
            if event.window_seconds is not None:
                switch_event = detect_language_switch(session_state, event, threshold=language_switch_threshold)
                session_state.confirm_language()
                if switch_event is not None:
                    session_state.record_language_switch_event(switch_event)
                    asyncio.create_task(session_state.cancel_all_lid_tasks())

            is_segment_language = event.window_seconds is None or (
                max_window_seconds is not None and event.window_seconds == max_window_seconds
            )
            if is_segment_language:
                session_state.record_segment_language(event)
                segment_start_sample = event.start_sample
                if event.language not in session_state.languages:
                    logger.info(
                        "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=segment_language_ignored | "
                        "reason=unsupported_language | window=%s | language=%s | confidence=%s | "
                        "boundary_sample=%s | boundary_seconds=%s",
                        session_state.session_id,
                        event.window_label,
                        event.language,
                        event.confidence,
                        segment_start_sample,
                        f"{(segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                    )
                elif event.confidence < language_switch_threshold:
                    logger.info(
                        "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=segment_language_ignored | "
                        "reason=below_threshold | window=%s | language=%s | confidence=%s | "
                        "boundary_sample=%s | boundary_seconds=%s",
                        session_state.session_id,
                        event.window_label,
                        event.language,
                        event.confidence,
                        segment_start_sample,
                        f"{(segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                    )
                elif event.language == session_state.current_language:
                    logger.info(
                        "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=segment_language_ignored | "
                        "reason=same_language | window=%s | language=%s | confidence=%s | "
                        "boundary_sample=%s | boundary_seconds=%s",
                        session_state.session_id,
                        event.window_label,
                        event.language,
                        event.confidence,
                        segment_start_sample,
                        f"{(segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                    )
                else:
                    logger.info(
                        "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=segment_language_ready | "
                        "from=%s | to=%s | confidence=%s | window=%s | boundary_sample=%s | boundary_seconds=%s",
                        session_state.session_id,
                        session_state.current_language,
                        event.language,
                        event.confidence,
                        event.window_label,
                        segment_start_sample,
                        f"{(segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                    )

        lid_component = getattr(components, "lid", None)
        if lid_component is not None and hasattr(lid_component, "classify"):
            lid_scheduler = ProgressiveLIDScheduler(
                lid_component,
                sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate,
                on_result=_handle_lid_result,
            )
        else:
            logger.warning(
                "session_id=%s | component=LID_scheduler | event=unavailable",
                session_id,
            )
        tail_silence_seconds = settings.asr_final_tail_silence_seconds
        token_duration_tolerance = settings.asr_token_duration_tolerance

        ready = ReadyMessage(session_id=session_id, languages=start.languages)
        await websocket.send_json(ready.model_dump())

        while True:
            message = await websocket.receive()

            if message.get("bytes") is not None:
                audio_bytes = message["bytes"]
                if not isinstance(audio_bytes, (bytes, bytearray, memoryview)):
                    raise InvalidAudioError("Audio bytes must be binary data")

                audio_bytes = bytes(audio_bytes)
                if not audio_bytes:
                    continue

                if len(audio_bytes) % 4 != 0:
                    raise InvalidAudioError("Audio bytes must be float32-aligned (len % 4 == 0)")

                if coordinator is None:
                    raise ComponentError("Coordinator", "Coordinator not initialized")

                try:
                    result = coordinator.process_audio_bytes(audio_bytes)
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise ComponentError(
                        "Coordinator",
                        f"Audio processing failed: {exc}",
                    ) from exc
                asr_result = result.asr_result
                for boundary_sample in result.boundaries:
                    boundary_seconds = boundary_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate
                    segment_start_sample = session_state.segment_start_sample
                    segment_start_seconds = segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate

                    if lid_scheduler is not None:
                        await lid_scheduler.finalize_segment(
                            session_state,
                            segment_start_sample=segment_start_sample,
                            segment_end_sample=boundary_sample,
                        )
                    else:
                        logger.warning(
                            "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=final_without_lid | "
                            "boundary_sample=%s | boundary_seconds=%s | reason=lid_unavailable",
                            session_state.session_id,
                            boundary_sample,
                            f"{boundary_seconds:.3f}",
                        )

                    switch_event = session_state.pop_latest_language_switch_event()
                    if switch_event is not None and switch_event.boundary_sample != segment_start_sample:
                        logger.info(
                            "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=switch_ignored | "
                            "reason=stale_segment | boundary_sample=%s | segment_start=%s | "
                            "boundary_seconds=%s | segment_start_seconds=%s",
                            session_state.session_id,
                            switch_event.boundary_sample,
                            segment_start_sample,
                            f"{(switch_event.boundary_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                            f"{segment_start_seconds:.3f}",
                        )
                        switch_event = None

                    if switch_event is None:
                        segment_language = session_state.latest_segment_language(segment_start_sample)
                        if (
                            segment_language is not None
                            and segment_language.language in session_state.languages
                            and segment_language.confidence >= language_switch_threshold
                            and segment_language.language != session_state.current_language
                        ):
                            switch_event = LanguageSwitchDetectedEvent(
                                session_id=session_state.session_id,
                                from_language=session_state.current_language,
                                to_language=segment_language.language,
                                confidence=segment_language.confidence,
                                window_seconds=segment_language.window_seconds,
                                window_label=segment_language.window_label,
                                boundary_sample=segment_start_sample,
                                detected_at_sample=boundary_sample,
                            )

                    if switch_event is not None and coordinator is not None and rollback_engine is not None:
                        asr_model, asr_stream, corrected = _apply_language_switch(
                            switch_event=switch_event,
                            session_state=session_state,
                            coordinator=coordinator,
                            rollback_engine=rollback_engine,
                            model_manager=model_manager,
                            fallback_models=components.asr_models,
                            sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate,
                            audio_end_sample=boundary_sample,
                            token_duration_tolerance=token_duration_tolerance,
                        )
                        if corrected is not None:
                            await websocket.send_json(corrected.model_dump())

                    tail_samples = round(tail_silence_seconds * sample_clock.sample_rate)
                    if tail_samples > 0:
                        try:
                            asr_stream.accept_samples(np.zeros(tail_samples, dtype=np.float32))
                            asr_stream.decode_available()
                        except Exception as exc:
                            logger.warning(
                                "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=tail_injection_failed | error=%s",
                                session_id,
                                exc,
                            )

                    final_result = asr_stream.finish_stream()
                    tokens = _build_token_timestamps(
                        final_result,
                        offset_seconds=segment_start_seconds,
                        max_end_seconds=boundary_seconds,
                    )
                    if final_result.text.strip() or tokens:
                        timestamp = tokens[0].start if tokens else segment_start_seconds
                        message = TranscriptionMessage(
                            type="final",
                            language=session_state.current_language,
                            text=final_result.text,
                            timestamp=timestamp,
                            tokens=tokens,
                        )
                        await websocket.send_json(message.model_dump())
                        session_state.add_transcription(
                            text=final_result.text,
                            type="final",
                            language=session_state.current_language,
                            sample_start=session_state.segment_start_sample,
                            tokens=_to_sample_tokens(tokens, sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate),
                        )

                    session_state.reset_segment(boundary_sample)
                    if lid_scheduler is not None:
                        lid_scheduler.schedule_windows(session_state, boundary_sample=boundary_sample)
                    if session_state.segment_start_sample != boundary_sample:
                        raise ComponentError(
                            "Coordinator",
                            f"Segment reset failed: expected {boundary_sample}, "
                            f"got {session_state.segment_start_sample}",
                        )

                    try:
                        asr_stream = asr_model.create_stream()
                    except Exception as exc:
                        raise ComponentError(
                            "ASR",
                            f"ASR stream initialization failed: {exc}",
                        ) from exc
                    coordinator.replace_asr_stream(asr_stream)
                    logger.info(
                        "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=segment_reset | "
                        "boundary=%s | boundary_seconds=%s | asr_stream_replaced=True",
                        session_id,
                        boundary_sample,
                        f"{boundary_seconds:.3f}",
                    )

                if result.boundaries:
                    continue

                pending_switch = session_state.pop_latest_language_switch_event()
                if pending_switch is not None:
                    if pending_switch.boundary_sample != session_state.segment_start_sample:
                        logger.info(
                            "session_id=%s | component=Coordinator | event=switch_ignored | "
                            "reason=stale_segment | boundary_sample=%s | segment_start=%s | "
                            "boundary_seconds=%s | segment_start_seconds=%s",
                            session_state.session_id,
                            pending_switch.boundary_sample,
                            session_state.segment_start_sample,
                            f"{(pending_switch.boundary_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                            f"{(session_state.segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate):.3f}",
                        )
                    else:
                        if coordinator is None or rollback_engine is None:
                            raise ComponentError(
                                "Coordinator",
                                "Switch handling unavailable (missing coordinator/rollback)",
                            )
                        asr_model, asr_stream, corrected = _apply_language_switch(
                            switch_event=pending_switch,
                            session_state=session_state,
                            coordinator=coordinator,
                            rollback_engine=rollback_engine,
                            model_manager=model_manager,
                            fallback_models=components.asr_models,
                            sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate,
                            audio_end_sample=session_state.sample_counter,
                            token_duration_tolerance=token_duration_tolerance,
                        )
                        if corrected is not None:
                            await websocket.send_json(corrected.model_dump())
                        continue

                if asr_result.text.strip() or asr_result.tokens:
                    segment_start_seconds = session_state.segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate
                    duration_seconds = session_state.sample_counter / sample_clock.sample_rate
                    tokens = _build_token_timestamps(
                        asr_result,
                        offset_seconds=segment_start_seconds,
                        max_end_seconds=duration_seconds + token_duration_tolerance,
                    )
                    timestamp = tokens[0].start if tokens else segment_start_seconds
                    message = TranscriptionMessage(
                        type="partial",
                        language=session_state.current_language,
                        text=asr_result.text,
                        timestamp=timestamp,
                        tokens=tokens,
                    )
                    await websocket.send_json(message.model_dump())
                    session_state.add_transcription(
                        text=asr_result.text,
                        type="partial",
                        language=session_state.current_language,
                        sample_start=session_state.segment_start_sample,
                        tokens=_to_sample_tokens(tokens, sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate),
                    )
                continue

            if message.get("text") is not None:
                text = message["text"]
                try:
                    finished = FinishedMessage.model_validate_json(text)
                except ValidationError:
                    await _send_error_and_close(
                        websocket,
                        code="INVALID_MESSAGE",
                        message="Invalid control message",
                        session_id=session_id,
                    )
                    return

                if finished.type == "finished":
                    if lid_scheduler is not None and session_state is not None:
                        await lid_scheduler.finalize_segment(
                            session_state,
                            segment_start_sample=session_state.segment_start_sample,
                            segment_end_sample=session_state.sample_counter,
                        )
                    if session_state is not None:
                        segment_start_seconds = session_state.segment_start_sample / sample_clock.sample_rate
                        end_seconds = session_state.sample_counter / sample_clock.sample_rate
                        tail_samples = round(tail_silence_seconds * sample_clock.sample_rate)
                        if tail_samples > 0:
                            try:
                                asr_stream.accept_samples(np.zeros(tail_samples, dtype=np.float32))
                                asr_stream.decode_available()
                            except Exception as exc:
                                logger.warning(
                                    "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | "
                                    "event=tail_injection_failed_on_finish | error=%s",
                                    session_id,
                                    exc,
                                )

                        final_result = asr_stream.finish_stream()
                        tokens = _build_token_timestamps(
                            final_result,
                            offset_seconds=segment_start_seconds,
                            max_end_seconds=end_seconds,
                        )
                        if final_result.text.strip() or tokens:
                            timestamp = tokens[0].start if tokens else segment_start_seconds
                            message = TranscriptionMessage(
                                type="final",
                                language=session_state.current_language,
                                text=final_result.text,
                                timestamp=timestamp,
                                tokens=tokens,
                            )
                            await websocket.send_json(message.model_dump())
                            session_state.add_transcription(
                                text=final_result.text,
                                type="final",
                                language=session_state.current_language,
                                sample_start=session_state.segment_start_sample,
                                tokens=_to_sample_tokens(tokens, sample_rate=sample_clock.sample_rate),
                            )
                    logger.info(
                        "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=session_finished",
                        session_id,
                    )
                    with suppress(Exception):
                        await websocket.close()
                    break

            if message.get("type") == "websocket.disconnect":
                break

    except ValidationError as exc:
        await _send_error_and_close(
            websocket,
            code="INVALID_CONFIG",
            message=_summarize_validation_error(exc, prefix="Invalid start message"),
            session_id=session_id,
        )
    except ConfigurationError as exc:
        await _send_error_and_close(
            websocket,
            code="INVALID_CONFIG",
            message=exc.message,
            session_id=session_id,
            unexpected=exc.__cause__ is not None,
        )
    except InvalidAudioError as exc:
        await _send_error_and_close(
            websocket,
            code="INVALID_AUDIO",
            message=exc.message,
            session_id=session_id,
            unexpected=exc.__cause__ is not None,
        )
    except ComponentError as exc:
        await _send_error_and_close(
            websocket,
            code="COMPONENT_FAILURE",
            message=f"{exc.component}: {exc.message}",
            session_id=session_id,
            unexpected=exc.__cause__ is not None,
        )
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        logger.info(
            "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=client_disconnected",
            session_id,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        await _send_error_and_close(
            websocket,
            code="COMPONENT_FAILURE",
            message=str(exc),
            session_id=session_id,
            unexpected=True,
        )
    finally:
        if session_state is not None:
            with suppress(Exception):
                await session_state.cancel_all_lid_tasks()
        if coordinator is not None:
            with suppress(Exception):
                coordinator.reset()
        with suppress(Exception):
            await websocket.close()

        logger.info(
            "session_id=%s | component=WebSocket | event=cleanup_complete",
            session_id,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.api.rest import router as rest_router
from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.api.websocket import router as websocket_router
from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.components import load_components


def _configure_logging() -> None:
    level_name = os.getenv("LR_LOG_LEVEL", "INFO").upper()
    level = getattr(logging, level_name, logging.INFO)
    logging.basicConfig(
        level=level,
        format="%(levelname)s: %(name)s: %(message)s",
    )


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    _configure_logging()
    app.state.components = load_components()
    yield


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(lifespan=lifespan)
    app.include_router(rest_router)
    app.include_router(websocket_router)

    frontend_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "frontend"

    @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    async def frontend_index() -> FileResponse:
        return FileResponse(frontend_dir / "index.html")

    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=frontend_dir), name="frontend")

    return app


app = create_app()

[0m$ [0mcd /home/runner/work/gladia-hunt-yuu/gladia-hunt-yuu && cat reposcan-raw/gladiaio/realtime-multilingual-asr-router/src/realtime_multilingual_asr_router/components.py
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.asr.kroko import KrokoASR
from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.asr.model_manager import ModelManager
from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.config import get_settings
from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.lid.voxlingua import VoxLinguaLID
from realtime_multilingual_asr_router.vad.silero import SileroVAD

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_KROKO_REQUIRED_FILES = ("encoder.onnx", "decoder.onnx", "joiner.onnx", "tokens.txt")


def _is_kroko_model_dir(path: Path) -> bool:
    if not path.exists() or not path.is_dir():
        return False
    return all((path / filename).exists() for filename in _KROKO_REQUIRED_FILES)


def _discover_kroko_language_paths(root: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """
    Discover Kroko model directories on disk, anchored off configured paths.

    This enables "drop in a model directory under models/streaming_transducers/<chunk_size>/<lang>" and have
    the API pick it up automatically without expanding Settings defaults.
    """
    if not root.exists() or not root.is_dir():
        return {}

    discovered: dict[str, Path] = {}
    for child in sorted(root.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name):
        if not child.is_dir():
            continue
        language = child.name
        if _is_kroko_model_dir(child):
            discovered[language] = child

    return discovered


@dataclass
class ComponentsRegistry:
    vad: SileroVAD | None = None
    asr_models: dict[str, KrokoASR] = field(default_factory=dict)
    asr_model_manager: ModelManager | None = None
    lid: VoxLinguaLID | None = None
    errors: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)


def load_components() -> ComponentsRegistry:
    settings = get_settings()
    registry = ComponentsRegistry()

    try:
        logger.info(f"component=VAD | event=load_start | path={settings.vad_model_path}")
        registry.vad = SileroVAD(model_path=settings.vad_model_path, sample_rate=16000)
        logger.info("component=VAD | event=load_success")
    except Exception as exc:
        message = str(exc)
        registry.errors["vad"] = message
        logger.error(f"component=VAD | event=load_failed | error={message}", exc_info=True)

    try:
        chunk_root = settings.asr_models_root / str(settings.asr_chunk_size)
        model_paths = _discover_kroko_language_paths(chunk_root)
        logger.info(
            "component=ASR | event=models_detected | root=%s | chunk_size=%s | languages=%s",
            chunk_root,
            settings.asr_chunk_size,
            sorted(model_paths.keys()),
        )
        if not model_paths:
            registry.errors["asr"] = "No ASR model directories found"
        else:
            registry.asr_model_manager = ModelManager(
                model_paths,
                sample_rate=settings.sample_rate,
            )
            registry.asr_models = {
                language: registry.asr_model_manager.get(language)
                for language in registry.asr_model_manager.languages()
            }
    except Exception as exc:
        message = str(exc)
        registry.errors["asr"] = message
        logger.error(
            f"component=ASR | event=load_failed | error={message}",
            exc_info=True,
        )

    try:
        logger.info(f"component=LID | event=load_start | path={settings.lid_model_path} | device=cpu")
        registry.lid = VoxLinguaLID(
            model_dir=settings.lid_model_path,
            device="cpu",
            sample_rate=16000,
        )
        logger.info(f"component=LID | event=load_success | path={settings.lid_model_path}")
    except Exception as exc:
        message = str(exc)
        registry.errors["lid"] = message
        logger.error(f"component=LID | event=load_failed | error={message}", exc_info=True)

    return registry

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{Mutex as TokioMutex, broadcast};
use tokio_tungstenite::tungstenite::protocol::Message;
use futures_util::{StreamExt, SinkExt};
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;
use base64::{Engine as _, engine::general_purpose::STANDARD};
use crate::vocabulary::{CustomVocabEntry, expand_vocabulary_for_languages, normalize_vocabulary};

const MAX_PENDING_AUDIO_BYTES: usize = 96_000; // ~3s at 16kHz/16-bit/mono

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct PendingAudioBuffer {
    chunks: VecDeque<Vec<u8>>,
    total_bytes: usize,
}

const EUROPEAN_LANGUAGE_CODES: &[&str] = &[
    "en", "es", "fr", "de", "it", "pt", "nl", "pl", "cs", "da", "sv", "no", "fi", "ro", "hu",
    "el", "tr", "uk", "ru",
];

fn resolve_session_languages(languages: Option<Vec<String>>) -> Vec<String> {
    if let Some(selected_languages) = languages {
        let filtered = selected_languages
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|lang| !lang.trim().is_empty())
            .collect::<Vec<String>>();
        if !filtered.is_empty() {
            return filtered;
        }
    }
    EUROPEAN_LANGUAGE_CODES
        .iter()
        .map(|lang| (*lang).to_string())
        .collect()
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct GladiaConfig {
    pub encoding: String,
    pub sample_rate: u32,
    pub bit_depth: u32,
    pub channels: u32,
    pub receive_partial: bool,
    pub receive_final: bool,
    pub languages: Vec<String>,
    pub code_switching: bool,
    pub endpointing: f64,
    pub custom_vocabulary: Vec<CustomVocabEntry>,
}

impl Default for GladiaConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            encoding: "wav/pcm".to_string(),
            sample_rate: 16000,
            bit_depth: 16,
            channels: 1,
            receive_partial: true,
            receive_final: true,
            languages: vec![],
            code_switching: false,
            endpointing: 0.6,
            custom_vocabulary: vec![],
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct GladiaClient {
    api_key: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<String>>>,
    ws_url: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<String>>>,
    session_id: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<String>>>,
    config: Arc<TokioMutex<GladiaConfig>>,
    ws_tx: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<tokio::sync::mpsc::UnboundedSender<Message>>>>,
    pending_audio: Arc<TokioMutex<PendingAudioBuffer>>,
    transcription_tx: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<broadcast::Sender<TranscriptionEvent>>>>,
    task_handle: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<JoinHandle<()>>>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
struct SessionRequest {
    encoding: String,
    sample_rate: u32,
    bit_depth: u32,
    channels: u32,
    endpointing: f64,
    messages_config: MessagesConfig,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    language_config: Option<LanguageConfig>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    realtime_processing: Option<RealtimeProcessing>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
struct RealtimeProcessing {
    custom_vocabulary: bool,
    custom_vocabulary_config: CustomVocabularyConfig,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
struct CustomVocabularyConfig {
    vocabulary: Vec<CustomVocabEntry>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
struct MessagesConfig {
    receive_partial_transcripts: bool,
    receive_final_transcripts: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
struct LanguageConfig {
    languages: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    code_switching: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct SessionResponse {
    id: String,
    url: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct ServerMessage {
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    message_type: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    data: Option<TranscriptionData>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct TranscriptionData {
    #[serde(default)]
    is_final: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    utterance: Option<Utterance>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct Utterance {
    text: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    start: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    end: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    speaker: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    confidence: Option<f64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub enum TranscriptionEvent {
    Partial(String),
    Final { text: String, start: f64, end: f64 },
    /// The session ended abnormally (network drop, protocol error). Carries a
    /// human-readable reason so the frontend can surface it. A normal,
    /// server-initiated close emits `SessionEnded` only — never `Error`.
    Error(String),
    SessionEnded,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum GladiaError {
    #[error("API key not set")]
    ApiKeyNotSet,
    #[error("HTTP error: {0}")]
    HttpError(#[from] reqwest::Error),
    #[error("WebSocket error: {0}")]
    WebSocketError(String),
    #[error("JSON error: {0}")]
    JsonError(#[from] serde_json::Error),
    #[error("Session not initialized")]
    SessionNotInitialized,
}

impl From<GladiaError> for String {
    fn from(error: GladiaError) -> Self {
        error.to_string()
    }
}

impl GladiaClient {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            api_key: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None)),
            ws_url: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None)),
            session_id: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None)),
            config: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(GladiaConfig::default())),
            ws_tx: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None)),
            pending_audio: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(PendingAudioBuffer::default())),
            transcription_tx: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None)),
            task_handle: Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None)),
        }
    }

    pub async fn init_session(
        &self,
        api_key: &str,
        languages: Option<Vec<String>>,
        code_switching: bool,
        custom_vocabulary: Vec<CustomVocabEntry>,
        endpointing: f64,
        region: &str,
    ) -> Result<String, GladiaError> {
        let normalized_vocabulary = normalize_vocabulary(custom_vocabulary);
        let resolved_languages = resolve_session_languages(languages.clone());

        // Guard: reuse an existing live session only when config is unchanged.
        {
            let config = self.config.lock().await;
            let session_unchanged = config.custom_vocabulary == normalized_vocabulary
                && config.languages == resolved_languages
                && config.code_switching == code_switching
                && config.endpointing == endpointing;
            drop(config);

            if session_unchanged {
                let existing = self.session_id.lock().await;
                if let Some(ref id) = *existing {
                    let alive = self.task_handle.lock().await
                        .as_ref()
                        .map(|h| !h.is_finished())
                        .unwrap_or(false);
                    if alive {
                        return Ok(id.clone());
                    }
                }
            } else {
                self.close_session().await?;
            }
        }

        *self.api_key.lock().await = Some(api_key.to_string());

        // Build the request body then release the lock before any awaits.
        let request = {
            let mut config = self.config.lock().await;
            config.code_switching = code_switching;
            config.languages = resolved_languages.clone();
            config.custom_vocabulary = normalized_vocabulary.clone();
            config.endpointing = endpointing;
            let api_vocabulary =
                expand_vocabulary_for_languages(normalized_vocabulary, &config.languages);
            let realtime_processing = if api_vocabulary.is_empty() {
                None
            } else {
                Some(RealtimeProcessing {
                    custom_vocabulary: true,
                    custom_vocabulary_config: CustomVocabularyConfig {
                        vocabulary: api_vocabulary,
                    },
                })
            };
            SessionRequest {
                encoding: config.encoding.clone(),
                sample_rate: config.sample_rate,
                bit_depth: config.bit_depth,
                channels: config.channels,
                endpointing: config.endpointing,
                messages_config: MessagesConfig {
                    receive_partial_transcripts: config.receive_partial,
                    receive_final_transcripts: config.receive_final,
                },
                language_config: if config.languages.is_empty() {
                    if config.code_switching {
                        Some(LanguageConfig {
                            languages: vec![],
                            code_switching: Some(true),
                        })
                    } else {
                        None
                    }
                } else {
                    Some(LanguageConfig {
                        languages: config.languages.clone(),
                        code_switching: if config.code_switching { Some(true) } else { None },
                    })
                },
                realtime_processing,
            }
        }; // config lock dropped here

        if let Some(ref realtime) = request.realtime_processing {
            let vocab = &realtime.custom_vocabulary_config.vocabulary;
            let unique_terms: std::collections::HashSet<&str> =
                vocab.iter().map(|entry| entry.value.as_str()).collect();
            let languages: std::collections::HashSet<&str> = vocab
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|entry| entry.language.as_deref())
                .collect();
            log::info!(
                "[gladiaflow] Live session custom vocabulary: {} entries ({} unique terms) across languages {:?}",
                vocab.len(),
                unique_terms.len(),
                languages
            );
        } else {
            log::info!("[gladiaflow] Live session request has no custom vocabulary");
        }

        let client = reqwest::Client::new();
        let response = client
            .post(format!("https://api.gladia.io/v2/live?region={region}"))
            .header("x-gladia-key", api_key)
            .header("x-gladia-version", format!("Gladiaflow/{}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .json(&request)
            .send()
            .await?;

        if !response.status().is_success() {
            return Err(GladiaError::WebSocketError(format!(
                "Failed to create session: {}",
                response.status()
            )));
        }

        let session: SessionResponse = response.json().await?;
        let session_id = session.id.clone();
        let ws_url = session.url.clone();

        log::info!("Gladia live session created (id={session_id})");

        *self.session_id.lock().await = Some(session_id.clone());
        *self.ws_url.lock().await = Some(ws_url);

        // Create broadcast channel for transcription events
        let (tx, _rx) = broadcast::channel::<TranscriptionEvent>(100);
        *self.transcription_tx.lock().await = Some(tx);

        // Start WebSocket connection
        self.start_websocket_task().await?;

        Ok(session_id)
    }

    pub async fn set_audio_format(
        &self,
        sample_rate: u32,
        channels: u32,
        bit_depth: u32,
    ) {
        let mut config = self.config.lock().await;
        config.sample_rate = sample_rate;
        config.channels = channels;
        config.bit_depth = bit_depth;
    }

    async fn start_websocket_task(&self) -> Result<(), GladiaError> {
        let ws_url = {
            let url = self.ws_url.lock().await.clone();
            url.ok_or(GladiaError::SessionNotInitialized)?
        };

        let (ws_stream, _) = tokio_tungstenite::connect_async(&ws_url)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| GladiaError::WebSocketError(e.to_string()))?;

        // Create channel for sending messages
        let (tx, mut rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<Message>();
        *self.ws_tx.lock().await = Some(tx);
        self.flush_pending_audio().await;

        // Split the stream
        let (mut sender, mut receiver) = ws_stream.split();

        let transcription_tx = self.transcription_tx.lock().await.clone();
        let session_id = self
            .session_id
            .lock()
            .await
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string());

        log::info!("[gladia-ws] websocket connected (session id={session_id})");

        // Store the handle so we can abort the task if needed
        let handle = tokio::spawn(async move {
            // Reason for an abnormal termination, surfaced to the frontend as an
            // Error event. Stays None for a clean, server-initiated close.
            let mut error_reason: Option<String> = None;
            loop {
                tokio::select! {
                    result = receiver.next() => {
                        match result {
                            Some(Ok(Message::Text(text))) => {
                                if let Ok(server_msg) = serde_json::from_str::<ServerMessage>(&text) {
                                    if let Some(data) = server_msg.data {
                                        if let Some(utterance) = data.utterance {
                                            if let Some(tx) = transcription_tx.as_ref() {
                                                let event = if data.is_final {
                                                    TranscriptionEvent::Final {
                                                        text: utterance.text,
                                                        start: utterance.start.unwrap_or(-1.0),
                                                        end: utterance.end.unwrap_or(-1.0),
                                                    }
                                                } else {
                                                    TranscriptionEvent::Partial(utterance.text)
                                                };
                                                let _ = tx.send(event);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    log::warn!("[gladia-ws] (session id={session_id}) unrecognised message: {text}");
                                }
                            }
                            Some(Ok(Message::Binary(_))) => {}
                            Some(Ok(Message::Ping(msg))) => {
                                let _ = sender.send(Message::Pong(msg)).await;
                            }
                            Some(Ok(Message::Close(frame))) => {
                                // Graceful close (normal stop, or a server-side
                                // session limit handled by reconnect upstream).
                                log::info!("[gladia-ws] (session id={session_id}) server closed: {frame:?}");
                                break;
                            }
                            Some(Err(e)) => {
                                log::error!("[gladia-ws] (session id={session_id}) websocket error: {e}");
                                error_reason = Some(format!("Connection error: {e}"));
                                break;
                            }
                            None => {
                                log::error!("[gladia-ws] (session id={session_id}) stream ended unexpectedly");
                                error_reason =
                                    Some("Connection to Gladia was lost unexpectedly".to_string());
                                break;
                            }
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                    Some(msg) = rx.recv() => {
                        if let Err(e) = sender.send(msg).await {
                            log::error!("[gladia-ws] (session id={session_id}) failed to send to ws, closing: {e}");
                            error_reason = Some(format!("Failed to send audio to Gladia: {e}"));
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    else => break,
                }
            }
            log::info!("[gladia-ws] (session id={session_id}) session ended");
            if let Some(tx) = transcription_tx.as_ref() {
                if let Some(reason) = error_reason {
                    let _ = tx.send(TranscriptionEvent::Error(reason));
                }
                let _ = tx.send(TranscriptionEvent::SessionEnded);
            }
        });
        
        *self.task_handle.lock().await = Some(handle);

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn test_connection(&self, api_key: &str) -> Result<bool, GladiaError> {
        let client = reqwest::Client::new();
        let response = client
            .get("https://api.gladia.io/v2/pre-recorded?limit=1")
            .header("x-gladia-key", api_key)
            .header("x-gladia-version", format!("Gladiaflow/{}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")))
            .send()
            .await?;

        Ok(response.status().is_success())
    }

    pub async fn current_session_id(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.session_id.lock().await.clone()
    }

    pub async fn send_audio(&self, audio_data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), GladiaError> {
        if audio_data.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let sender = self.ws_tx.lock().await.clone();
        if let Some(tx) = sender {
            let payload = serde_json::json!({
                "type": "audio_chunk",
                "data": {
                    "chunk": STANDARD.encode(audio_data),
                }
            });

            tx.send(Message::Text(payload.to_string()))
                .map_err(|e| GladiaError::WebSocketError(e.to_string()))?;
        } else {
            self.enqueue_pending_audio(audio_data.to_vec()).await;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn enqueue_pending_audio(&self, chunk: Vec<u8>) {
        let mut pending = self.pending_audio.lock().await;
        pending.total_bytes += chunk.len();
        pending.chunks.push_back(chunk);

        while pending.total_bytes > MAX_PENDING_AUDIO_BYTES {
            if let Some(dropped) = pending.chunks.pop_front() {
                pending.total_bytes = pending.total_bytes.saturating_sub(dropped.len());
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn flush_pending_audio(&self) {
        let sender = self.ws_tx.lock().await.clone();
        let Some(tx) = sender else {
            return;
        };

        let queued_chunks = {
            let mut pending = self.pending_audio.lock().await;
            pending.total_bytes = 0;
            pending.chunks.drain(..).collect::<Vec<Vec<u8>>>()
        };

        for chunk in queued_chunks {
            let payload = serde_json::json!({
                "type": "audio_chunk",
                "data": {
                    "chunk": STANDARD.encode(&chunk),
                }
            });
            if tx.send(Message::Text(payload.to_string())).is_err() {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    /// Signal Gladia to stop recording. The server will process remaining
    /// audio buffers, send final transcriptions, and then close the WebSocket.
    pub async fn stop_recording(&self) -> Result<(), GladiaError> {
        let sender = self.ws_tx.lock().await;
        if let Some(tx) = sender.as_ref() {
            let msg = serde_json::json!({"type": "stop_recording"});
            tx.send(Message::Text(msg.to_string()))
                .map_err(|e| GladiaError::WebSocketError(e.to_string()))?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn subscribe_to_transcriptions(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<TranscriptionEvent> {
        let tx = self.transcription_tx.lock().await;
        if let Some(ref tx) = *tx {
            tx.subscribe()
        } else {
            // Return a dummy receiver if no session
            let (_, rx) = broadcast::channel(1);
            rx
        }
    }

    pub async fn close_session(&self) -> Result<(), GladiaError> {
        // Drop the sender so the WS task's rx closes, then abort the task.
        // (stop_recording already sent the graceful stop before this is called.)
        *self.ws_tx.lock().await = None;

        if let Some(handle) = self.task_handle.lock().await.take() {
            handle.abort();
            // Wait for abort to complete; ignore the expected Cancelled error.
            let _ = handle.await;
        }

        *self.ws_url.lock().await = None;
        *self.session_id.lock().await = None;
        *self.pending_audio.lock().await = PendingAudioBuffer::default();
        *self.transcription_tx.lock().await = None;
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Default for GladiaClient {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

## Summary

**No reportable findings this cycle.** All hits are either:
1. **Test fixtures** in vercel-ai (a fork of Vercel AI SDK with Gladia provider)
2. **Security controls** (SSRF protection denying 169.254.169.254, cross-origin credential stripping on redirects)
3. **Previously triaged** items (orphaned npm package, CSP gap, OAuth redirect_to reflection)

The Gladia org's own repos (sdk, gladia-cli, n8n-nodes-gladia, gladiaflow, realtime-multilingual-asr-router) show clean security practices:
- Proper API key handling via `x-gladia-key` header
- Environment variable fallbacks (`GLADIA_API_KEY`)
- Secure config file permissions (0600)
- Cross-origin credential stripping on redirects (both JS and Python SDKs)
- No SSRF vulnerabilities (self-hosted ASR router uses local models only)
